500 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA, FEBRUARY 1, 2025 (MAGHA 12, 1946) [PARTIV 


मेट्रॉपॉलिटन Yelm एक्सचेंज ऑफ इंडिया लिमिटेड 


पंजीकृत कार्यालय : बिल्डिंग ए, यूनिट 205ए, दूसरी मंजिल, पीरामल अगस्त्य कारपोरेट पार्क, एल.बी.एस. रोड, Galt 
पश्चिम मुम्बई-400 070 


भारतीय प्रतिभूति और विनिमय बोर्ड (सेबी) से अनुमोदन प्राप्त करने के अनुसरण में एमएसई नियमों को निम्नानुसार संशोधित किया 
जाता है :- 


नियम 
1. अध्याय- 1 बोर्ड 2क) 


अध्याय - 1 के शेयरधारक निदेशकों से संबंधित नियम 2क को निम्नानुसार आशोधन द्वारा संशोधित किया जाता हैः 


2क शासी बोर्ड की संरचना निम्नानुसार होगी : 


क) गैर-स्वतंत्र निदेशक, लोक हित निदेशकों की संख्या से अधिक नहीं होंगे; 
ख) लोक हित निदेशक; और 
ग) प्रबंध निदेशक | 

2. अध्याय- 1 बोर्ड 2 घ) 


अध्याय - 1 के प्रबंध निदेशक से संबंधित नियम 2घ) को निम्नानुसार आशोधन द्वारा संशोधित किया जाता हैः 


प्रबंध निदेशक शासी बोर्ड में पदेन निदेशक होगा और उसे गैर-स्वतंत्र निदेशक की श्रेणी में शामिल किया जाएगा। 


3. अध्याय -1 बोर्ड 2 च) 


अध्याय - 1 के शेयरधारक निदेशक से संबंधित नियम 2 च) को निम्नानुसार आशोधन द्वारा संशोधित किया जाता है:

घोषित किया गया था, उसके लिए प्राप्त प्रतिफल संबंधित प्राधिकरण द्वारा डिफॉल्टर्स संबंधित समिति को भुगतान किया जाएगा,

द्वारा ट्रेड सेटलमेंट गारंटी फंड को उसके द्वारा देय या भुगतान योग्य निर्धारित की गई हैं और क्लियरिंग कॉर्पोरेशन के नियमों,

अध्याय |, खंड 1.3 - क्लियरिंग बैंक

aR क्लियरिंग बैंक के रूप में नियुक्त किए जावे वाले बैंक और ऐसे बैंक जिन्हें क्लियरिंग कॉर्पोरेशन धन निपटान एजेंसी के रूप में 
कार्य करने, सभी सौदों के लिए मार्जिन धन एकत्र करने और क्लियरिंग सदस्यों के बीच तथा क्लियरिंग सदस्यों और क्लियरिंग

Pursuant to approval received from Securities and Exchange Board of India (8581), MSE Rules are amended as 
hereunder: — 


RULES 
1. Chapter- I Board 2A) 
Rule 2A pertaining to Shareholders Directors of Chapter I is amended by way of modification as under: 
2A The Composition of the Governing Board shall be as under: 
a) Non-Independent Director not exceeding the number of Public Interest Directors; 
b) Public Interest Directors; and 
c) Managing Director. 
2. Chapter I Board 2D) 
Rules 2D) pertaining to Managing Directors of Chapter I is amended by way of modification as under: 


The Managing Director shall be an ex-officio director on the governing board and shall be included 
in the category of Non-Independent Director. 


3. Chapter I Board 2F) 
Rules 2F) pertaining to Shareholder Directors of Chapter I is amended by way of modification as under: 


Non-Independent Director shall be elected from the shareholders of the Company except Trading Members 
and their associates and agents. 


4. Chapter I Board 2G) 
Rules 2G) pertaining to Shareholder Directors of Chapter I is amended by way of modification as under: 


Any employee of the Company appointed on the governing board in addition to the Managing Director, shall 
be deemed to be a Non-Independent Director. 


THE GAZETTE OF INDIA, FEBRUARY 1, 2025 (MAGHA 12, 1946) [PARTIV 


5. Chapter I Board 21) 


Rules 2) pertaining to Shareholder Directors of Chapter I is amended by way of modification as under: 


The Appointment including re-appointment of Non-Independent Director to the governing board shall be 
with prior approval of SEBI. 


DURGESH KADAM 
Head-Legal & Company Secretary 
Metropolitan Stock Exchange of India Limited 


Indian Clearing Corporation Limited 


Registered Office: Indian Clearing Corporation Limited, 15th Floor, P. J. Towers, Dalal Street, Fort, Mumbai — 400 001 


Rules of Indian Clearing Corporation Limited 


A. Whereas pursuant to SEBI Circular dated June 25, 2024 on “Statutory Committees at Market 
Infrastructure Institutions (MIIs)’, it is proposed to amend the Rules of Indian Clearing Corporation 
Limited (“ICCL”), as under, inviting public comments in terms of Securities Contracts (Regulation) Act, 1956: 


1) 


2) 


Chapter III, Clause 22 (m) — Nomination 


When the Relevant Authority has exercised the right of nomination in respect of a membership 
which was vested in the Clearing Corporation upon a member having been declared a defaulter 
on or subsequent to the date to be specified by the Relevant Authority as referred to in clause (b) 
of this Sub-rule, the consideration received therefore shall be paid by Relevant Authority to the 
Defautters> concerned Committee to be applied for the purposes and in the order of priority 
specified in the Bye-Laws and the Regulations of the Clearing Corporation. 


Chapter IV, Clause 44 (b) — Consequences of Cessation of Membership Right by reason of Death 


When a Clearing Member dies, all subscriptions, debts, fines, fees, charges and other monies as 
shall have been determined by the Relevant Authority to be due by him to the Clearing 
Corporation, all amounts as shall have been determined by the Defautters* concerned Committee 
to be due or payable by him to the Frade-Settlement Guarantee Fund and all debts, liabilities, 
obligations and claims arising out of any contracts made by him subject to the Rules, Bye-laws 
and Regulations of the Clearing Corporation as shall have been admitted by the Relevant 
Authority shall be paid and satisfied in full before his legal representatives or heirs are allowed to 
exercise the right of nomination.

Chapter I, Clause 1.23 — Interpretation 


(i) All other words and expressions used but not defined in these Bye-laws, but defined in the 
Rules and Regulations of the Clearing Corporation, BSE (Corporatisation & Demutualisation) 
Scheme, 2005, SCRA, SEBI Act, Companies Act, 2013, Depositories Act, 1996 or in the Rules 
and Regulations made thereunder shall have the same meaning respectively assigned to them in 
such Rules and Regulations, Acts or the Rules or the Regulations made thereunder or the Scheme 
or any statutory modification or re- enactment thereto, as the case may be; 


(ii) In case a term is defined in more than one of the above, then its meaning as defined in that, 
which precedes the others mentioned hereinabove, shall prevail, unless necessitated otherwise. 


(iii) Headings shall not affect the construction or interpretation of any Bye-law: 


(iv) Words importing masculine gender shall include feminine gender and vice versa and neutral 
gender in the case of companies, corporations, firms, etc, unless the context otherwise requires. 


Chapter II, Clause 2.2 — Clearing Segments 


Without prejudice to generality of the Bye-law 2.1, the Clearing Corporation has created separate 
Clearing Segments for Equity, Debt, Currency Derivatives, Commodity Derivatives and Equity 
Derivative Segment of the Specified Exchange and-Currency Derivative Seementof United Stock 
Exchance-ofindia-Limited. 

Chapter IV, Clause 4.2 (f) — Regulations 


Prescription from time to time and administration of penalties, fines and other consequences 
including suspension/expulsion of Clearing Members from the Clearing Corporation for 
defaults/violations/non-compliances; 


Chapter XII — Clause 12.5, 2 - Composition of Fund 


Any penalties levied by CC (as per Regulation 34-0f SECC Regulations) shall be credited to 
Core SGF corpus.
